# Tokens: Buttons (TOKEN_TABLE.1c4257f8804f9478)

Source: <https://m3.material.io/_dsm/data/dsdb-m3/2026-09-23_06-10-05/TOKEN_TABLE.1c4257f8804f9478.json>, design system 'Google Material 3' revision 7989 (2026-05-27T22:44:15.009106Z).
Used on: <https://m3.material.io/components/buttons>

Each token lists its value per context. `->` shows the reference chain (component token -> system token -> reference token); the resolved value is what the baseline scheme produces in that context. Contexts with identical chain and value are merged.

## md.comp.button — Button (baseline)

This token set is no longer recommended. Baseline GM3 tokens for the button. For expressive, make the button using a size (md.comp.button.large) and color (md.comp.button.filled) token set.

  - 32dp
- **md.comp.button.large.label-text** (Button large label size)

## md.comp.button.small — Button - Size - Small

For small buttons.

- **md.comp.button.small.container.height** (Button small container height)
  - 40dp
- **md.comp.button.small.container.shape.round** (Button small shape round)
  - -> md.sys.shape.corner.full = circular
- **md.comp.button.small.container.shape.square** (Button small shape square)
  - -> md.sys.shape.corner.medium = rounded_corners 12dp
- **md.comp.button.small.icon-label-space** (Button small between icon label space)
  - -> md.sys.measurement.space100 = 8dp
- **md.comp.button.small.icon.size** (Button small icon size)
  - 20dp
- **md.comp.button.small.label-text** (Button small label size)
  - [Audience=3P + Font=Variable + Platform=Compose; Audience=3P + Font=Variable + Platform=Web; Audience=3P + Font=Variable + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.typescale.label-large = Roboto Flex; weight 500; fontSize 14pt; lineHeight 20pt; fontTracking 0pt; axes wdth=100 wght=500 slnt=0 opsz=14 GRAD=0 ROND=0 CRSV=0 HEXP=0 FILL=0
  - [Audience=3P + Font=iOS bold + Platform=Compose; Audience=3P + Font=iOS bold + Platform=Web; Audience=3P + Font=iOS bold + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.typescale.label-large = Roboto; weight 700; fontSize 14pt; lineHeight 20pt; fontTracking 0.1pt
  - [Audience=3P + Font=Static + Platform=Compose; Audience=3P + Font=Static + Platform=Web; Audience=3P + Font=Static + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.typescale.label-large = Roboto; weight 500; fontSize 14pt; lineHeight 20pt; fontTracking 0.1pt
- **md.comp.button.small.leading-space** (Button small leading space)
  - -> md.sys.measurement.space200 = 16dp
- **md.comp.button.small.outlined.outline.width** (Button small outline width)
  - 1dp
- **md.comp.button.small.pressed.container.corner-size.motion.spring.damping** (Button small shape spring animation damping)
  - [Motion scheme=Expressive + Platform=Compose; Motion scheme=Expressive + Platform=Web; Motion scheme=Expressive + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.motion.spring.fast.spatial.damping = 0.6
  - [Motion scheme=Standard + Platform=Compose; Motion scheme=Standard + Platform=Web; Motion scheme=Standard + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.motion.spring.fast.spatial.damping = 0.9
- **md.comp.button.small.pressed.container.corner-size.motion.spring.stiffness** (Button small shape spring animation stiffness)
  - [Motion scheme=Expressive + Platform=Compose; Motion scheme=Expressive + Platform=Web; Motion scheme=Expressive + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.motion.spring.fast.spatial.stiffness = 800
  - [Motion scheme=Standard + Platform=Compose; Motion scheme=Standard + Platform=Web; Motion scheme=Standard + Platform=Android]: -> md.sys.motion.spring.fast.spatial.stiffness = 1400
- **md.comp.button.small.pressed.container.shape** (Button small shape pressed morph)
  - -> md.sys.shape.corner.small = rounded_corners 8dp
- **md.comp.button.small.selected.container.shape.round** (Button small selected container shape round)
  - -> md.sys.shape.corner.medium = rounded_corners 12dp
- **md.comp.button.small.selected.container.shape.square** (Button small selected container shape square)
  - -> md.sys.shape.corner.full = circular
- **md.comp.button.small.trailing-space** (Button small trailing space)
  - -> md.sys.measurement.space200 = 16dp

## md.sys.color

- **md.sys.color.inverse-on-surface** (Inverse on surface) — Used for text and icons shown against the inverse surface color
- **md.sys.color.inverse-surface** (Inverse surface) — Displays opposite color of the surrounding UI
- **md.sys.color.on-primary** (On primary) — Text and icons shown against the primary color
- **md.sys.color.on-secondary** (On secondary) — Text and icons shown against the secondary color
- **md.sys.color.on-secondary-container** (On secondary container) — Contrast-passing color shown against the secondary container
- **md.sys.color.on-surface** (On surface) — Text and icons shown against the surface color
- **md.sys.color.on-surface-variant** (On surface variant) — For text and icons to indicate active or inactive component state
- **md.sys.color.outline** (Outline) — Subtle color used for boundaries
- **md.sys.color.outline-variant** (Outline Variant) — Outline-variant is used to define the border of a component where 3:1 contrast ratio isn’t required, a container, or a divider.
- **md.sys.color.primary** (Primary) — Main color used across screens and components
- **md.sys.color.secondary** (Secondary) — Accent color used across screens and components
- **md.sys.color.secondary-container** (Secondary container) — Less prominent container color, for components like tonal buttons
- **md.sys.color.shadow** (Shadow) — For shadows applied to elevated components
- **md.sys.color.surface-container** (Surface Container)
- **md.sys.color.surface-container-low** (Surface Container Low)
- **md.sys.color.surface-tint** (Surface tint)
